# BC-250 discovery inventory

All 971 repository IDs from the ten-query snapshot of 2026-09-29. This is a search inventory, not 971 endorsed or necessarily related projects. Zero-star repositories and forks are retained. Titles/descriptions and raw README bodies are deliberately not copied.

[English catalog](en/README.md) · [Каталог по-русски](ru/README.md) · [Каталог українською](uk/README.md)

## Reading the columns

- **Catalog** links to an editorial topic when this repository has been described there; a dash means no editorial entry yet, not rejection.
- **Signal** is the older lexical triage, not a relevance verdict. `explicit-board-term` can come from an inherited README. `no-readme`, `empty-repo` and `sensitive-excluded` refer to the stored snapshot.
- **Fork / archived** are GitHub flags at the snapshot. README identity alone cannot establish identical source code. Every fork retains its own URL and revision.
- **Source** links to a pinned revision when available. A dash means the inventory had no revision; do not construct one.
